# LEDGER — the every-string ledger (package 25)

Consolidated per plan.md §4. Columns: string key/file:line · route/surface · state · current text · keep/change/cut/conditional · final text · factual prerequisite · owner · original finding IDs · checked evidence.

Rows come from two sources, both visible per row:
1. **Package-reviewed rows** — carried verbatim (verdict, final text, prerequisite, owner, finding IDs, evidence) from the per-package ledgers (`docs/audits/v3-consolidated/ledger/{05,08,11,12,13,14,15,16,18,19,20,21,22,23}-strings.md`) where a package already reviewed the string. Owner is `package N`.
2. **Scan-derived rows** — every remaining candidate string `tools/string-ledger-scan.mjs` found under this surface's mapped files, not yet claimed by any package ledger. Verdict is `keep` (owner `package 25 review`) only for an unambiguous, rule-safe short UI verb/status word (Save, Cancel, Close, Sign in, …); `flagged` when a plan §4 rules-check pattern matched (see the Rules check report section — these are NOT fixed here, only listed as a work item for the owning package); otherwise `unreviewed` — package 25 did not have the domain context to render a verdict on a sentence carrying a specific factual claim (a prerequisite, a state description, an organizer-authored fact) without doing another package's investigation over again. **The unreviewed count is the headline number** — see `docs/audits/v3-consolidated/reports/25-string-ledger.md`.

Organizer-authored body text (public information, regulations text, seed demo copy) is listed once as **content, not chrome** in its own subsection per surface, per plan §4's instruction not to globally rewrite it.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ModuleCatalogRow.tsx` `module-impact-{id}` (ownsData=true) | Settings → Modules row | module enabled, has data | "Owns operational data; existing matches or draws are preserved. Review them before disabling." | change | "{Name} has draws or matches, so it stays on. It can be turned off once they are removed through {Name}." | `_module_has_data` (meet→matches, bracket→bracket_events) really blocks disable with 409 `MODULE_HAS_DATA`; DTO only exposes a boolean `hasData`, not counts, so counts are not claimed | package 05 | V3-OC26.1 | `apps/api/src/workspaces/workspace_modules.py:95-219`, `core/schemas.py` `WorkspaceModuleDTO` |
| `ModuleCatalogRow.tsx` `module-impact-{id}` (ownsData=false) | Settings → Modules row | module has no data | "Display settings do not own match data." / "No operational records are stored yet." | change | "Turning {Name} off hides it from this workspace's navigation. Nothing is stored yet, so nothing is deleted." | Disabling a data-free module is a pure status flip (`repo.modules.update`); nothing is ever deleted by disable | package 05 | V3-OC26.1 | same route file, PATCH handler body |
| `ModuleCatalogRow.tsx` `module-completion-{id}` (status disabled) | Settings → Modules row | disabled | "Off; existing data preserved" | change | "Off" | A disabled module with data cannot exist per the has-data guard (data blocks disabling); the phrase implied a state that can't occur, and duplicated the impact line | package 05 | V3-OC26.1 | same |
| `ModuleCatalogRow.tsx` modal body (`Review {name} data impact`) | Settings → Modules → Review impact modal | opened from a has-data row | "This module owns operational data in this workspace. Disabling it could hide or invalidate those records, so the server keeps it enabled until the data is cleared through its normal workflow. No data will be removed here." | change | "{Name} has draws or matches, so it stays on. It can be turned off once they are removed through {Name}." (same sentence as the row) | Same as above — modal must state the same consequence as the row (ruling) | package 05 | V3-OC26.1 | same |
| `ModulesSettingsTab.tsx` `blockedReason` (hasData branch) | Settings → Modules row (title attr / extra reason line) | module enabled, has data | "This module has matches or draws. Clear its data before disabling it." | cut | (removed — row's impact line already states the consequence; button branch is "Review impact", not a disabled "Disable" button, so this reason was redundant and instructed deletion-to-disable) | Ruling: never instruct the user to delete data in order to disable | package 05 | V3-OC26.1 | `ModuleCatalogRow.tsx` render branches: `ownsData` renders "Review impact", not "Disable" |

| `SeasonStatusCell.tsx` `chip-open` cell | Discovery calendar row, entries open | `closesAt` present | "Entries open · closes in 15d" | change | "Closes 30 Aug 2026, 13:00 GMT+1 · 5d" (exact tournament-timezone instant primary, relative days secondary) | New `SeasonRowDTO.closesAt`/`timeZone` (backend) carry the exact instant; `formatMomentInZone` (D11, the entrant time authority) renders it | package 21 | V3-PE01.2 | `apps/api/src/entries/entries_json.py` `SeasonRowDTO`, `apps/entrant/app/components/SeasonStatusCell.tsx` |
| same cell | Discovery calendar row, entries open, no parseable exact instant | any | (unchanged) | keep | "Entries open · closes in Nd" (fallback, unchanged copy) | Rule 4: degrade to what is known rather than omit the countdown entirely | package 21 | V3-PE01.2 | `openDeadlineLabel` fallback branch |
| `SeasonCalendar.tsx` `CalendarRow` meta line | Discovery calendar row | venue address parses to 2+ comma segments | "Kingsway Centre" (venue only) | change | "Kingsway Centre · London, United Kingdom" | New `SeasonRowDTO.locality`, a best-effort "City, Country" line out of the free-text venue address (no structured city/country column exists — D2) | package 21 | V3-PE01.3 | `apps/api/src/entries/entries_json.py` `_locality`, `apps/entrant/app/components/SeasonCalendar.tsx` |
| same | Discovery calendar row | address has no comma / is absent | (unchanged) | keep | venue name only — no invented locality | Rule 4: omit, never guess | package 21 | V3-PE01.3 | `_locality` returns `None` |

# Prerequisites still open

Every `conditional` verdict (or a `debt`/limitation note that leaves a factual claim
un-satisfiable today) found across the package ledgers, with what closes it and who owns
closing it. None of these are fixed by package 25 — this table exists so the conditional
verdict is not lost between packages.

| Verdict source | String / finding | Factual prerequisite | Closes when | Owner |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| V3-PE15.1 (package 22, ruling R2) | Organizer-authored "report N minutes before match is called" rule, regulations reader | `apps/api/src/workspaces/setup.py`'s public-information section has no reporting-time/check-in reference field to cite — package 22 left the organizer's rule untouched rather than inventing a system-chrome cross-reference | Package 13 adds a structured reporting-time/check-in reference field to Setup → Public information, and the organizer confirms/fills it | Package 13 (field) then package 22 (cites it next to the rule, still without rewriting the rule) |
| Package 05 debt (A4) | `enter.tsx:333` partner-invite hint — "We'll try to email them an invitation…" | `_send_partner_invite` can fail and there is no entrant-facing surface (resend/edit-entry route) to report or recover from a failed partner invite | An entries-flow package adds a partner-invite resend/status surface (receipt page or My entries) | Not yet assigned — logged to `docs/reference/debt-log.md`; candidate for package 24 (My entries / receipt scope) |
| Package 13 debt | `ScoringFields.tsx` "Deuce (win by 2)" / point cap | `pointCap` was added only to the Setup `rules` section's `TournamentConfig`-adjacent document; the Meet/Bracket Engine Config tab (`EngineConfigForm.tsx`) reads a separate `TournamentConfig` document that does not yet carry `pointCap`, so the same fact can drift between the two surfaces | A decision + change widens `EngineConfigForm`'s `ScoringValue` contract to read the same `pointCap`, or the two are explicitly reconciled | Package 13 (owns Setup's `pointCap`) with package 09/10 (owns Engine Config) |
| Package 15 debt (V3-OC13.2, partial) | `SetupProduct.tsx:442` Logo/Banner/Regulations preview-failure message | No URL-syntax validation exists on these fields, so a malformed URL and a reachable-but-broken image both render the same "could not be loaded" message; the acceptance asks for distinct messages | Field-level URL format validation is added to the Public information form | Package 13/15 |
| Package 08 debt | `SharingTab.tsx` invite-mode radio rows | Hit area not measured against the 24px AA minimum; no finding currently pins it | A future accessibility pass measures and, if needed, enlarges the rows | Package 08/26 |
| Package 23 debt (V3-PE23.2) | Turnstile test-widget copy ("HUMAN CHECK" etc.) on the signup form | This package can only observe the dev/test-sitekey widget; the copy is Cloudflare-controlled and production behavior needs a real, non-test sitekey in a release environment to capture | A release-environment capture is taken | Package 27 (evidence closure) |

# Rules check

Plan §4's table applied row by row against the ledger above (both package-reviewed and
scan-derived rows). Violations are listed as a **work list for the owning package** — none
are edited here.

| §4 rule | Checked how | Result |
|---|---|---|
| Page titles: sentence case, one destination, matches linked destination | Scanned every `jsx-text`/`title`/`aria-label` string shaped like a page heading (h1/h2 context inferred from surrounding component name) | **One violation:** `apps/entrant/app/routes/schedule.tsx:641` — page `<h1>` reads `"Schedule / Live"`, a slash-assembled compound of two destination names rather than one page title. Work item for the owner of PE09 (package 04/11's schedule surface; no open package currently owns a copy-only revisit — flag for whichever package next touches `schedule.tsx`). |
| Buttons/links: verb + object, promise the actual result | Spot-checked all `jsx-text` inside `<button>`/`<a>` contexts captured by the scan (`kind: jsx-text` on short imperative strings) | No bare non-verb button labels found in the scanned corpus beyond the pre-existing `SAFE_SHORT` allow-list (Save/Cancel/Close/etc., themselves verbs or established idioms). No further violations found; a full per-button destination audit (does "X" really navigate to what it promises) is beyond a string-shape scan and is verification-matrix (plan §6) territory, not this ledger. |
| Field labels: persistent, familiar language, units where needed | Cross-checked scan `prop:label`/`prop:hint` entries against plan §4's own example ("Minimum rest between matches", "32 pairs" vs "32 players") | Package 13 already applied the exact unit-noun rule (V3-OC09.1, V3-OC10.1). No further un-audited bare-number-with-no-noun label found in the scan corpus (the `bare_number_only` and `(n)`-shaped regex swept the full 1,315-entry scan and found zero). |
| Helpers: decision-relevant, next to the control | Not scan-checkable (requires DOM adjacency, not string shape) | Not verified by this tool — leave to per-surface visual QA (plan §6 verification matrix), out of a string-scan's reach. |
| Status: one domain fact, no routine reassurance | Grepped scan corpus for `ago_relative`/`tbd`/`na_bare` patterns | Zero hits — prior packages (05, 12) already removed "TBD", relative-only "ago" phrasing, and bare "N/A" from the audited scope. |
| Dates/numbers: format centrally, show tz/year, counts have nouns | Grepped scan corpus for raw-ISO date shapes, bare parenthesized counts, and bare "+N" | Zero hits in the current scan (packages 04a/11/12/19/20 already redirected date/count formatting to the shared authorities). No open violation found; a raw-ISO/bare-count regression would now have to reappear in new code to violate this rule. |
| People/matches: names first, no slash-assembled pairs, stable sides | Ran the `slash-pair-names` pattern (`^[A-Z]\w+\s*/\s*[A-Z]\w+$`) across the full scan corpus | **One hit**, the same `"Schedule / Live"` string above — a page-title compound, not a person/side pair (no genuine slash-joined name found; D14's `sideSummaryPhrase` seam already replaced the person-join case per package 11). Listed once under both rows to avoid a reader missing it under either category. |
| Empty states: one explanation + a usable next step | Not scan-checkable (requires a redundancy comparison, not string shape) | Not verified by this tool. Packages 12/14/15/22 already reviewed the empty states in their scope (OC02, OC32/OC33, publication, PE16) — the remaining unreviewed empty-state copy (e.g. any not yet touched by a package) is in the `unreviewed` bucket above by surface, not separately flagged here. |
| Errors: say what failed + recovery; preserve input | Not scan-checkable (requires behavior verification) | Not verified by this tool — plan §6 verification-matrix territory. |
| Success: only verified outcomes | Not scan-checkable | Not verified by this tool — package 05/23's mail-outcome work already addressed the highest-risk instances (signup/verify/reset). |
| Destructive/recovery copy: what changes, what's retained, reversibility | Not scan-checkable | Package 19 already reviewed Backup/Restore copy against this rule (V3-OC27.2). |
| Accessibility text: names action + target; status without color alone | Cross-checked `prop:aria-label`/`prop:ariaLabel` entries for generic non-descriptive labels (e.g. bare `"button"`, `"icon"`, `"click here"`) | Zero hits for the generic-label anti-pattern in the scan corpus. Full contrast/color-only verification is plan §6 territory, not a string scan. |
| Organizer content: preserve facts, edit chrome separately | Confirmed no scan entry is sourced from `regulationsText`/public-information body fields (those are server-stored organizer content, never a string literal in `apps/console`/`apps/entrant` source — the scan tool cannot and should not see them) | Organizer body text is out of the scan's reach by construction; package 22's C1/C2 rows are the ledger's only coverage of it, listed under PE15 as **content, not chrome**. |
| Hidden/interactive copy: loading/focus/validation/success/empty/permission states | Scan includes a `toast:*` kind (none found — this product has no `toast()`/`notify()` call sites) and a `dom-text` kind for the browser-twin assets | 10 `dom-text` entries were captured (all under `apps/entrant/public/assets/`) and are listed under their mapped surface, mostly `unreviewed` — see the per-surface tables. |
